## Best date plans

### 2026-09-25 — Bryant Park Square Dance + Ambassadors Clubhouse

**Bryant Park Square Dance, then dinner.** North Indian / Punjabi at Ambassadors Clubhouse. It is a 12-minute walk from the venue. It runs to 9 pm, so this is a late stop afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Bryant+Park%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&destination=1245+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/square-dance/2026-09-25) · [Restaurant](https://ambassadorsclubhouse.com/)

### 2026-09-25 — Double Windsor + The History of Concrete

**Dinner, then The History of Concrete.** Pub / American at Double Windsor. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. The History of Concrete starts at 7:10 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=210+Prospect+Park+W%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&destination=188+Prospect+Park+W%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/movies/the-history-of-concrete/) · [Event booking](https://nitehawkcinema.com/prospectpark/purchase/24331635/) · [Restaurant](https://www.doublewindsor.com/)

### 2026-09-29 — Pretty in Pink x Straight White Men Can’t Dance + Ambassadors Clubhouse

**Pretty in Pink x Straight White Men Can’t Dance, then dinner.** North Indian / Punjabi at Ambassadors Clubhouse. It is 1.2 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 6:30 pm, so eat afterwards.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=323+6th+Ave%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10014&destination=1245+Broadway%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10001&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.ifccenter.com/) · [Restaurant](https://ambassadorsclubhouse.com/)

### 2026-09-26 — Last Saturdays: Open Classroom + Sofreh

**Last Saturdays: Open Classroom, then a meal.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 1.8 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 12 pm, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=750+5th+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11232&destination=75+St+Marks+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.green-wood.com/event/last-saturdays-open-classroom-4/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-09-25 — Evelina + Sarah Davachi

**Dinner, then Sarah Davachi.** Italian / Mediterranean at Evelina. It is a 24-minute walk from the venue. Sarah Davachi starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=211+Dekalb+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11205&destination=119+Pierrepont+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://lpr.com/lpr_events/sd26nyc/) · [Event booking](https://kyd.to/0mFYTPkb) · [Restaurant](https://www.evelinabk.com/)

### 2026-09-28 — Rucola + Broadway Nights Off: Betsy Wolfe and Adam Kantor

**Dinner, then Broadway Nights Off: Betsy Wolfe and Adam Kantor.** Northern Italian at Rucola. It is 2.1 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Doors 6:30 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=190+Dean+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&destination=20+Battery+Pl%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10280&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://bpca.ny.gov/event/broadway-nights-off-betsy-wolfe/) · [Restaurant](https://rucolabrooklyn.com/)

### 2026-09-27 — Smorgasburg Prospect Park + Grimm Tavern

**Smorgasburg Prospect Park, then a meal.** Brewpub / American at Grimm Tavern. It is 1.3 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 11 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Breeze+Hill%2C+Prospect+Park+%28enter+at+Lincoln+Road%29%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&destination=659+Vanderbilt+Ave%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.smorgasburg.com/) · [Restaurant](https://www.theinfatuation.com/new-york/reviews/grimm-tavern)

### 2026-09-28 — Sofreh + A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan•American

**Dinner, then A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan•American.** Persian at Sofreh. It is 2.0 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. A Winged Victory for the Sullen with Pan•American starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.

## What to do this week

### 2026-09-25

#### [Bryant Park Square Dance](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/square-dance/2026-09-25)

6:00 PM · dance · Free · Bryant Park · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 71.0  
Bryant Park's annual square dance features a line-dancing lesson, live band and square dancing, trick roping, lassoing lessons, a mechanical bull, and frontier-style crafts.
**Timing:** 6-9pm
**Practical note:** Outdoors; NWS forecasts a slight chance of showers after 5pm, gusts near 32 mph, and likely rain later, so confirm before going and bring rain gear.
**Why now:** Annual free square dance on Friday evening.
[Official](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/square-dance/2026-09-25) · [Tickets / booking](https://bryantpark.org/calendar/event/square-dance/2026-09-25) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/fri-mon-9-25-28-skint-weekend/) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/)

#### [14th Annual MoRUS Film Festival: Resistance](https://www.eventbrite.com/e/14th-annual-morus-film-festival-spyborg-tickets-1998438711984)

7:00 PM · workshop · Free; donations welcome · 6&B Garden · East Village · Manhattan · Score 69.0  
The surveillance-themed MoRUS festival presents a Friday resistance program with Cypurr Collective's intro security-planning workshop, Seth Tobocman live narration, and activist technology films.
**Timing:** Friday night program begins at 7pm in 6&B Garden; rain location is MoRUS at 155 Avenue C.
**Practical note:** Outdoor garden program; NWS forecasts rain likely Friday night, so check the Eventbrite update for a move to the MoRUS rain location.
**Why now:** The Friday program is one night only within the festival's September 24-26 run.
**On-sale watch:** Outdoor program has a rain location and may update by Eventbrite email.

6:00 PM · unusual · $15-$400 · Green-Wood Cemetery · Greenwood Heights · Brooklyn · Score 51.0  
Death of Classical and Green-Wood partner with the Metropolitan Opera for a cemetery walk, tastings, performances, excerpts, and a creative-team conversation for the new opera Lincoln in the Bardo.
**Timing:** Immersive walk 6-7:30pm; mainstage preview 7:30-8:30pm; return walk by 9pm.
**Practical note:** Outdoors on hilly cemetery paths; NWS forecasts rain likely Friday night, and the official rain date is Saturday, September 26.
**Why now:** One-night Green-Wood preview before the opera's October world premiere at the Met.
**Hot booking alert:** The official listing notes some seating tiers are sold out and all tickets are final.

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 54.6  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.
[Official](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

#### [Pop-Up Audubon: Autumn Bird-Safe Window Clings](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/pop-up-audubon-i-11/2026-09-27/)

12:00 PM · outdoors · Free · Prospect Park · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 70.0  
Prospect Park Alliance naturalists host a free Pop-Up Audubon session on autumn migration, with leaf window-cling making to help reduce bird-window collisions and a short nature walk.
**Timing:** Drop-in activities noon-4pm; Nature on the Go walk 3-4pm.
**Practical note:** Outdoor drop-in; official listing cancels for rain, extreme heat, or very unhealthy air, and NWS forecasts rain Sunday.
**Why now:** Autumn migration theme is scheduled for this Sunday in Prospect Park.
**On-sale watch:** Weather may cancel the drop-in program.

## Where to eat

Verified against captured restaurant/menu evidence and limited follow-up checks; kept current, source-backed offers and reclassified ordinary menus/openings where needed.

Dining research: 2026-09-25T10:34:20+00:00

No kept item relies on an unsourced Michelin-star claim. Eater’s El Califa de León Michelin-star context appears only inside rejected/aggregate heatmap material, and Kirbee’s source background mentions Michelin honors for Texas pitmasters but the kept Kirbee’s card does not present an NYC Michelin award claim.

### Weekday bar-and-lounge aperitivo menu

Deals & happy hours — Ci Siamo

Useful pre-dinner Midtown option: the official menu lists aperitivo-only bar/lounge items including pizza bianca, pizzette, gnocco fritto, mortadella, prosciutto, salads, and potatoes.

Price: Aperitivo items shown at $14-$27; tuna crudo price unknown  
Schedule: Monday-Friday, 3:00-4:30 pm  
Conditions: Official Ci Siamo menu lists Aperitivo in the bar & lounge Monday-Friday, 3:00-4:30 pm; aperitivo items are $14-$27 where priced, with tuna crudo unpriced on the captured menu. Source: https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Sky-high lunch prix fixe

Special menus — Manhatta

A splurge-with-a-view lunch option: the official menu lists two- and three-course lunch formats with dishes such as hamachi crudo, dry-aged burger, sea bass, pork neck, duck leg confit, and desserts.

Price: Two courses $65; three courses $78  
Schedule: Lunch schedule not stated on fetched menu  
Conditions: Official Manhatta lunch menu lists Two Course | 65 and Three Courses | 78; lunch days/hours are not stated in captured menu data. Source: https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/)

### Midday $2 oyster window

Deals & happy hours — The Dutch

Useful between-meals seafood stop: the official Midday menu lists oysters on the half shell at $2 each alongside deviled eggs, calamari, burger, fries, and fried chicken.

Price: Oysters on the half shell $2 each; other midday items $11-$36  
Schedule: Monday-Sunday, 3:00-5:00 pm  
Conditions: Official Midday menu lists Monday-Sunday, 3:00-5:00 pm, with oysters on the half shell $2 each; varieties/limits are not stated. Source: https://www.thedutchnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.thedutchnyc.com/menus/)

### Le Rock brasserie lunch at Rockefeller Center

More restaurants — Le Rock

Midtown option for a la carte lunch before museums/theater: official menu lists chickpea panisse, omelette with caviar, moules frites, cheeseburger deluxe, and other brasserie standards.

Price: Lunch items checked include cheeseburger deluxe $32, moules frites $32, omelette $28, sides $12; not a special discount  
Schedule: Opening hours in official structured data: Monday-Friday 11:00-21:00, Saturday 16:00-21:00  
Conditions: Ordinary restaurant menu, not a special menu or discount; official schema lists hours Monday-Friday 11:00-21:00 and Saturday 16:00-21:00. Source: https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.lerocknyc.com/menus/)
